1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the molecules object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 206. The moduli space has dimension at most 962 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to molecules theory, we decompose the problem into 206 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 962. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
